### 2026-09-09 - DATA-004/AGENT-002 normalized revenue evidence, local only

Autumn requested the next task after #357. This increment owns only the DATA-004 revenue adapter and AGENT-002 integration, not acquisition storage, signup-to-payment instrumentation or ActiveCampaign onboarding. The isolated worktree began on `codex/318-post-merge-checkpoint` at #357 merge `3e2f012`, with only the unpublished release note dirty. It was preserved in local commit `397e1c4`, then reconciled with fresh main `e6935834d34afa393f399c9288ba66755f9db18b` on `codex/318-revenue-evidence` at `a245901`. The sole conflict was independent ledger appends: both histories and the newer AC rows were retained in full. No reset, clean, stash or shared-checkout edit occurred. The AC task received a coordination message retaining its ownership.

Implementation commit: `f39db434f8b3eafb421821a37f6dfa8221d65225`. `adaptRevenueEvidence` accepts strictly allowlisted, bounded normalized snapshots and emits the existing `MetricSnapshot` contract for the Revenue Agent. It performs no provider request, credential access, database read/write or persistence. It exports no endpoint and is not wired into an owner trigger, durable workflow, schedule, model or Production runtime. No dependencies, member UI, entitlements, acquisition events or AC assets changed.

The contract deliberately separates:

- **Captured collection:** successful, paid, positive, verified single-capture evidence, in integer minor units for one Stripe account, test/live mode, currency and closed UTC capture date. An active plan, paid invoice, authorization-only payment, zero payment, unknown capture date or cumulative multi-capture amount is not substituted for collection evidence.
- **Retained capture-cohort amount:** captured amount minus successful refunds observed for those charges, excluding fees. Unresolved refunds, disputes, incomplete refund coverage, conflicting or cross-charge refund IDs and unsafe arithmetic withhold this value. It is not a daily refund-flow total, net cash, accounting revenue or a bank payout. Different-aged cohorts require interpretation; arithmetic comparison alone does not establish a business driver.
- **Membership attribution:** complete, fresh Outseta account/subscription evidence and explicit verified payment allocations, observed after the capture cohort closes. Email/metadata-only links, ambiguous customers, wrong account/mode/subscription, orphan allocations and over-allocation withhold membership totals. Shared Agency accounts are not multiplied by member count. Current cancellation or plan state is reported separately from historical collection; an old subscription pointer that cannot be reconciled remains unknown.
- **Balance availability:** pending, available or unknown in Stripe, retaining the balance currency separately. None means money reached a bank. MRR and ARR remain null until a recurring-subscription policy and suitable evidence are approved. No confirmed-upgrade event is invented.
- **Observation history:** identical input and duplicate/reordered evidence retain deterministic results; changed source observations receive immutable revision keys. Future persistence must choose the newest verified observation for each cohort and never sum revisions. This increment does not change the projection-store upsert or write anything to it.

The payments skill and independent official-document review informed these distinctions: [Stripe charge semantics](https://docs.stripe.com/api/charges/object), [balance transactions](https://docs.stripe.com/api/balance_transactions/object), [refund outcomes](https://docs.stripe.com/api/refunds/object), [versioned invoice-payment allocations](https://docs.stripe.com/changelog/basil/2025-03-31/add-support-for-multiple-partial-payments-on-invoices), and [Outseta account/person structure](https://go.outseta.com/support/kb/articles/aWxJOqQV/sync-outseta-users-to-your-database). This is a repository normalization contract, not a claim that a provider API returns it directly. Caller labels such as approved export, complete coverage and verified allocation are assertions that a future separately approved collector must independently substantiate.

**Actually passed at f39db43:** source TypeScript compilation; API TypeScript compilation; all **127/127 Runtime Node tests**, including **25** new `revenue-evidence.test.mjs` cases and the incoming saved-profile onboarding cases; runtime format check; specialist static validation; Preview runtime/error-mapping static guards; whitespace check. The new tests cover successful/partial captures, successful/pending/failed refunds, disputed charges, unknown balance, empty versus incomplete evidence, freshness/date boundaries, identity/account/currency/mode conflicts, duplicate and contradictory records, Agency attribution, unsupported payload fields, safe-integer overflow, immutable observation/replay keys and adapter-to-Revenue-Agent comparison. Review identified and corrected pre-capture membership observations, mutable retained-snapshot keys and falsely combined source authority before the suite passed. A local command session delayed completion but ultimately exited successfully; it was not a test failure or hosted verification.

**Not run and not claimed:** Workflow provider integration (no workflow code or wiring changed), new Preview acceptance, live Stripe/Outseta reads, bank-payout reconciliation, database tests or writes, signed-in member/browser smoke, Next.js Production build, emails, AC actions, credentials/configuration changes, model calls or schedules. Previous #357 hosted evidence remains tied to `6644f31`. No migration was added or applied. The DATA-004 row is `validated_local`; AGENT-002 retains its older synthetic `verified_preview` with the new source path explicitly local-only.

Next dependency: implement bounded GET-only provider normalization and latest-observation selection locally; document exact account/resource/PII and freshness scope before requesting a live connection. Membership truth remains Outseta, payment outcomes remain Stripe, conversion storage remains acquisition-owned, and ActiveCampaign remains marketing rather than financial authority. No recurring-revenue policy, new access rule or approval exception is introduced here.

Canonical structure/base coupling, rejection self-tests and whitespace checks passed after this ledger reconciliation. Fresh pruned main remained `e693583` immediately before publication. Publication is a focused review PR, not a direct-main write, merge, Production rollout or live-source authorization.

Published as draft [PR #359](https://github.com/autumn2busy/nested-objects-starter/pull/359), `codex/318-revenue-evidence` to main. This records code and local validation only; no hosted acceptance or deployment success is inferred from PR creation.

### 2026-09-09 - DATA-004/AGENT-002 scoped readers and immutable snapshot selection

Autumn approved continuing the next local implementation step. This increment stays on `codex/318-revenue-evidence` and extends existing draft #359 rather than creating a second PR. Starting HEAD was clean `79dfb47d9d89d517b88a325123fd3ab70c28eacd`; fresh pruned main remained `e6935834d34afa393f399c9288ba66755f9db18b`. GitHub reported all #359 Runtime, ledger and Vercel contexts successful at that starting SHA. Those are CI/deployment records, not source-connected acceptance. The DATA-004/AGENT-002 ownership and AC/acquisition boundaries are unchanged.

`BillingReadOnlyClient` implements explicit-ID GETs for a Stripe charge, its separately approved refund collection, an approved balance transaction, invoice-payment allocations for an approved invoice, and an approved Outseta account. Policy limits cover reviewed account/hostname, record/resource IDs, expected test/live mode, fixed tested Stripe API version `2025-06-30.basil`, review expiry, request/page counts, deadlines and response size. Fixture mode requires an injected transport and refuses credentials; network mode additionally requires explicit enablement and supplied server-side credentials. No environment variable is read, no request happens at construction/import, and no application endpoint, scheduled job or existing service enables this client. Review validity is checked before and after requests. Errors are sanitized, redirects forbidden, caching disabled, pages validated atomically and incomplete reads retain partial/unavailable status. Response provenance retains the provider tenant/account, expected mode, version and review reference without credentials.

Returned observations strip contact, card, address, email and arbitrary metadata fields. Outseta requests a fixed minimal fields list; its documented `StripeId` is retained only as a candidate customer link, with identity still unknown until the connected Stripe account and payment relationship are independently reconciled. A nullable `CurrentSubscription` is not replaced by `LatestSubscription`, and stage/plan IDs are not translated into a new access policy. A successful explicit-ID read is not a complete business-day collection: every result retains `captureDateCoverage: unknown`. Charge creation and balance posting timestamps are never renamed capture timestamps. Invoice allocations retain their own amount/payment reference; they are not automatically labeled verified membership payments. These limits are required before the normalized adapter can safely consume live records.

`selectRevenueSnapshots` selects one in-memory decision view per metric/date/account/mode/currency/source-kind/capture cohort without summing immutable revisions. It validates adapter provenance, unit, source authority and idempotency consistency. Newest unknown, undated, stale, future or conflicting evidence cannot silently fall back to an older healthy value. It checks every relevant source timestamp, keeps incomplete groups explicitly unknown, preserves deterministic replay behavior including correlation tie-breaks, and withholds different payloads reused under one immutable key. Its output is a decision view for the existing Revenue Agent, not another persisted adapter observation, a new database, or an automatic projection-store write.

The payments skill and current primary documentation guided the reader shapes: [Stripe charge retrieval](https://docs.stripe.com/api/charges/retrieve), [refund pagination](https://docs.stripe.com/api/refunds/list), [invoice-payment listing](https://docs.stripe.com/api/invoice-payment/list), [Outseta account retrieval](https://docs.outseta.com/api-reference/crm/retrieve-an-account), [Outseta OpenAPI schema](https://docs.outseta.com/api-reference/openapi.json) and [minimal field selection](https://go.outseta.com/support/kb/articles/d1QpxQE7/outsetas-rest-api). A successful charge GET cannot independently establish exact capture time, single-capture provenance or full-day population. No new daily-revenue, settlement, conversion or entitlement claim is enabled by these readers.

Live acceptance still requires a concrete reviewed provider account, explicit IDs/resources, credential handling, test/live selection, PII and freshness scope. Complete capture-date population and verified capture/allocation evidence remain separate prerequisites for business-wide revenue conclusions. Database writes, member-facing changes, Production rollout, models, schedules, email and marketing mutations remain on hold. No credential, real billing record or provider account was accessed in this local increment.

Implementation commit `f69cb3c20a97a036d3d34230b480e1dc5ba5a13a`: **174/174 Runtime Node tests passed** after final source/API compilation, including 32 `billing-readonly.test.mjs` cases, 15 `revenue-snapshot-selection.test.mjs` cases and the prior 127 cases. Source/API no-emit checks, runtime format, specialist/Preview/error-mapping static checks and whitespace passed. The tests use injected transports or a local fetch stub with dummy strings; no provider network calls or real credentials were used. The source-review context omission and deterministic correlation tie-break were corrected before final verification; immutable-key payload conflicts and review expiry after a response are also covered. No Next.js Production build or new hosted/Workflow/database/member-browser acceptance was run. Existing #357 and earlier #359 evidence retain their original SHA and environment boundaries.
